營收走勢圖

玻璃陶瓷–電子材料  
富喬(1815.TT) 
AI 材料升級趨勢確立，高階玻纖布開啟成長新局

富喬為台灣少數具備紗、布垂直整合與自有配方能力的玻纖布廠。2H26 
受惠二代低介電布放量、報價調漲及良率改善，營收與 獲利可望逐季創
高；進入 2027 年後，獲利成長動能將由高階產品占比提升與新增產能
貢獻。台灣廠持續將既有產能轉向二代低介電布，東莞廠自 1Q27 起分
批將 E 布產能升級至二代布，泰國一期則自 2H27 起逐步投產，承接
客戶對非中國高階產能的需求，加上低膨脹布開始導入，產品組合將進
一步往高階化發展。研究部預估富喬 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 
5.13 元與 9.30 元，稅後淨利分別年增 267.9% 與 81.4%。考量富喬
為日系龍頭以外少數具備垂直整合與自有配方能力的供應商，以  2027 
年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 185 元，初次給予買進
投資評等。

1. 規格升級推動產品組合高階化 
AI 伺服器與高速交換器的傳輸速率持續提升 ， 板材由 M7、M8 向 M9 以
上升級，玻纖布同步由 E-glass 轉向一代、二代低介電布。石英布因鑽
孔與除膠渣良率受限而導入遞延，終端客戶轉回採用二代低介電布，延
長其產品週期。研究部預估富喬二代低介電布營收占比將由 2025 年的 
7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 51%。

2. 既有機台重新配置，毛利率大幅跳升 
玻纖布織布機由豐田獨家供應 ， 新機交期已排至 2031 年，2027 年以前
產業新增供給主要仰賴既有機台重新配置 。 富喬現有約 920 台織機 ， 憑
藉紗、布垂直整合，可在無須大幅增加資本支出下將既有產能轉向二代
低介電布 ， 帶動毛利率由 2025 年的 27.6%， 升至 2026 年的 44.7% 與 
2027 年的 54.8%。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 2

公司簡介 
富喬工業（1815 TT）成立於 1999 年，為台灣少數具備玻璃纖維紗至玻璃纖
維布垂直整合能力的材料廠，主要產品涵蓋電子級、工業級玻纖紗及電子級玻
纖布，其中電子級玻纖布為銅箔基板（CCL）與印刷電路板（PCB）的關鍵上游
材料。公司目前主要生產基地位於雲林斗六、虎尾及中國東莞，並已布局泰國
新產能，以強化非中國供應鏈配置。相較單純織布廠，富喬具備自有玻纖紗生
產與配方研發能力，可依終端規格變化調整紗種與產品組合。近年受惠 AI 伺
服器、高速交換器等應用帶動 PCB 材料規格持續升級，富喬營運重心逐步由
傳統 E-glass 轉向低介電等高階電子級材料。

玻纖布產業介紹 
玻纖布位於 PCB 材料供應鏈最上游，製程橫跨玻璃熔煉與紡織兩個領域。玻
纖原料經窯爐熔融後 ， 由鉑銠漏板拉製成單絲 ， 再經上漿 、 集束與加撚形成電
子紗 ， 後續以織機織成玻纖布 ， 並經開纖及矽烷偶聯劑等後處理後 ， 交由 CCL 
廠含浸樹脂製成膠片，再與銅箔壓合成銅箔基板，最終應用於 PCB 或載板。 
玻纖布規格主要可由織物型號 、 紗徑與玻璃配方三個面向區分 。 以織物型號而
言，7628 屬厚布，基重約 210 g/m²，2116、1080 依序變薄，10 系列則屬超
薄布；若依紗徑區分，粗紗單纖直徑約 9 至 13 微米，細紗則降至約 4 至 
9 微米，隨紗徑變細，斷絲風險與製程難度同步提高，織機亦須降速，超薄布
生產效率僅約厚布的二分之一至三分之一 。 另一方面 ， 玻璃配方則直接決定材
料電性 ， 介電損耗亦隨材料世代持續下降 。 隨高速傳輸規格提升 ，玻纖與樹脂
之間的介電差異會放大訊號時延與織紋效應 ， 因此傳輸速率每提升一個世代 ，
材料亦須同步升級。 
除玻璃配方外，織布後的開纖與表面處理亦是高階玻纖布性能的關鍵。即使
CCL 廠商採用相同原料，不同玻纖布廠的產品在電性、耐熱性與良率上的表現
仍可能存在顯著差異，也使高階玻纖布認證週期通常長達兩年以上。就材料成
本結構而言，玻纖布占傳統 CCL 材料成本約 25% 至 40%，若升級至石英布，
占比可提高至約六成；隨高階 PCB 對 CCL 規格與用量提升，CCL 占 PCB 用
料成本比重亦由過去三至四成提升至五成以上。玻纖布因此已由過去的通用材
料，逐步轉變為影響高速板材性能、成本與供應穩定性的關鍵材料。

富喬為台灣少數具備玻纖紗、布
垂直整合與自有配方能力的材料
廠，近年受惠 AI 帶動高階 PCB 
材料升級 ， 營運重心正由傳統 E-
glass 轉向低介電玻纖布，並同
步布局泰國非中國產能。

玻纖布位於 PCB 材料供應鏈最
上游 ， 製程與認證門檻高 ， 且隨高
速傳輸推升材料規格升級，其成
本占比與對板材性能的重要性同
步提升，已由通用材料轉為高階  
PCB 的關鍵材料。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

AI 世代推升高速材料規格持續升級 
AI 基礎建設對材料的需求，並非僅來自伺服器出貨量成長，更重要的是運算
密度與傳輸速率持續提升，帶動 PCB 材料規格同步升級。相較傳統伺服器約
三至四年的平台週期，AI 伺服器迭代速度明顯加快，GPU、ASIC 與交換器間
的資料傳輸需求快速增加，推動網路架構由 400G、800G 向 1.6T、3.2T 演
進。隨訊號速率提升，傳輸損耗與訊號完整性要求同步提高，板材須持續降低
介電常數與介電損耗，帶動 CCL 由 M7、M8 向 M9 以上升級；玻纖布作為影
響板材電性的重要材料，亦由傳統 E-glass 逐步升級至一代、二代低介電
布，並進一步朝石英布與第三代低介電材料發展。 
就目前材料演進路徑而言，400G 交換器主要採用 M7 板材搭配一代低介電
布，800G 升級至 M8 後，一代與二代低介電布開始並用；進入 1.6T 世代
後，二代低介電布的重要性進一步提升，部分高階規格則開始評估導入石英
布。展望 2028 年後，隨 3.2T 逐步導入，材料規格預期將進一步朝升級版石
英布或第三代低介電布發展，高階玻纖布的滲透率持續提升。 
研究部認為，規格升級將成為本輪玻纖布產業成長的核心驅動力。隨 AI 運算
與高速傳輸規格持續演進，材料需求將由傳統 E-glass 加速轉向低介電、低
膨脹等高階產品。在終端平台持續更替的背景下，玻纖布須隨板材世代同步升
級，而每一次規格提升亦伴隨 ASP 與單位產值增加，使產業成長不僅來自需
求量擴張，更來自產品組合持續高階化。

表二、CCL 等級與對應玻纖布

資料來源：統一投顧整理

升，帶動 PCB 材料由 E-glass 
向低介電、低膨脹等高階玻纖布
升級，推升高階產品滲透率、ASP 
與單位產值，成為產業成長的核
心驅動力。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

專利與認證構成高階玻纖布的實質進入障礙 
高階玻纖布的核心技術門檻在玻纖紗配方，低介電與低膨脹材料的關鍵專利亦
主要集中於紗端，後進者若要切入，須重新設計配方以迴避既有專利，良率與
量產時程因而受限。另一方面，CCL 廠需將特定玻纖布與樹脂、銅箔共同調
校，一旦納入合格供應商名單，更換材料通常須重新開立料號並進行完整驗
證，使認證週期可長達數年。富喬憑藉紗、布垂直整合，可直接由紗端調整配
方與製程，並已完成多地高階低介電玻纖布專利布局，低膨脹布亦已突破既有
專利限制並通過終端認證。研究部認為專利保護與長認證週期共同構成高階玻
纖布的實質進入障礙，亦使中國廠商產能雖快速擴張，短期內能實際轉化為高
階產品供給的比例仍有限。

供需失衡與料源綁定 
玻纖布供需失衡自 2026 年起由通用布延伸至高階布，主要瓶頸集中於織機、
良率與認證。玻纖布織機由豐田獨家供應，新機交期已排至 2031 年，且超薄
與特殊布須降速生產，使名目機台數無法等比轉化為實際產出；另一方面，高
階布認證週期普遍超過兩年，也使既有產能難以在短期內快速切換料號。 
E 布短缺主要來自龍頭廠商退出低階產能。日東紡、旭化成陸續停產 E-glass 
布後，供給收縮帶動各類 E 布報價自 2025 年底至 2026 年普遍上漲逾一倍
以上。惟中國新增產能自 2027 年陸續開出，加上消費性需求仍偏弱，研究部
預期 E 布供需將於 2027 年逐步緩解，報價漲幅趨緩。 
一代低介電布供需相對平衡，惟隨二代布需求放大，具備雙線能力的廠商傾向
將織機轉向二代布。二代低介電布則為目前供給最為吃緊的品項，主要對應 
M8 以上板材。除織機供給受限外，良率更是限制有效產出的關鍵，新進者量
產良率多不足五成，使名目產能與實際出貨量存在明顯落差，也支撐二代布報
價維持在一代布的約 2 至 3 倍。 
低膨脹布的供給缺口則可能延續更久。隨先進封裝尺寸放大與層數增加，而供
應仍高度集中於日東紡，新產能最快自 2H27 起陸續開出。石英布則面臨不同
瓶頸，問題不在供給，而在加工良率與導入進度；由於鑽孔與除膠渣難度較
高，部分原規劃採用石英布的設計轉回二代低介電布，反而延長二代布的產品
週期。 
供給吃緊亦同步改變材料認證與採購模式。過去玻纖布多由 CCL 廠與板廠選
料，現階段雲端業者與系統廠已逐步直接認證布廠並指定料源，部分客戶另要
求非中國產能，CCL 廠亦透過長約提前鎖定高階布供給。高階玻纖布的競爭因
而由單純產能擴張，轉向認證、良率與料源掌握能力的競爭。

高階玻纖布具備配方專利與長認
證週期雙重門檻，限制新進者有
效產能釋放，也使富喬憑藉紗布
垂直整合與既有認證基礎建立明
顯競爭優勢。

玻纖布供給受限於織機、良率與
認證門檻，高階產品短缺延續並
推動客戶直接綁定料源，產業競
爭 重 心 由 產 能 擴 張 轉 向 認 證 能
力、量產良率與供應穩定性。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

圖三、二代低介電布供需缺口

資料來源：統一投顧預估

產能配置與產品組合 
富喬目前共有約 920 台織布機，其中台灣 490 台、東莞 430 台。受限於織
布機交期，富喬 2027 年以前的主要成長動能，是將既有機台由 E 布、一代
低介電布持續轉向單位產值與毛利率更高的二代低介電布，透過產品組合升級
放大既有產能效益。 
台灣廠部分機台維持生產 E 布，供應台系 CCL 廠與低軌衛星應用，其餘產能
則持續轉向二代低介電布，低膨脹布亦預計自 2027 年起逐步導入。海外方
面，東莞現有 430 台於 2026 年仍以 E 布為主，自 1Q27 起採台灣供紗、當
地織布模式分批轉作二代低介電布。泰國一期則規劃新增 500 台織機，預計
自 2H27 起分批到位，主要生產低介電布並承接客戶對非中國產能的需求。 
富喬能快速進行跨廠區與跨產品配置，關鍵在於紗、布垂直整合。公司自有電
子級玻纖紗可隨布種升級同步調整紗的配方與規格，降低對外部紗源的依賴。
隨台灣產能持續高階化、東莞轉產二代布及泰國新產能加入，研究部預估二代
低介電布營收占比將由 2025 年的 7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 
51%，同期毛利率亦由 27.6% 升至 44.7% 與 54.8%。

富喬以既有機台轉產高毛利二代
低介電布，並透過東莞轉線與泰
國新產能擴充高階產品供給，帶
動二代布營收占比與整體毛利率
持續提升。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 7

圖四、富喬產品組合演變（營收占比）

資料來源：統一投顧預估

營運概況 
1. 2H26 營運將改寫新高 
富喬 7、8 月營收分別為 8.54 億元（+24.0% MoM、+68.1% YoY）與 9.12 億
元（+6.8% MoM、+79.2% YoY） ，連續改寫單月新高。隨新一代 AI 伺服器與 
1.6T 交換器平台陸續放量，二代低介電布需求同步攀升，加以 6 月起調漲的
報價自 3Q26 全季認列，研究部預估 3Q26 營收 27.66 億元（+29.8% QoQ、
+80.4% YoY） ，改寫單季歷史新高。毛利率升至 48.8%（+8.8ppt QoQ、
+21.9ppt YoY） ，除產品組合與報價效益外，二代布量產良率亦已逐步改善。
營業利益 10.39 億元（+89.8% QoQ、+301.5% YoY） ，營益率 37.6%；稅後淨
利 8.58 億元（+1.8% QoQ、+203.2% YoY），EPS 1.40 元，EPS 較 2Q26 小幅
下滑係因盈餘轉增資後股數增加。展望 4Q26，隨著二代布出貨比重持續提升
以及良率續揚貢獻，研究部預估營收 30.51 億元（+10.3% QoQ、+86.4% 
YoY） 、毛利率 49.9%、EPS 1.62 元。

2. 2027 年成長動能轉向產品組合升級，EPS 估 9.30 元 
2027 年富喬獲利成長動能將由價格上漲轉向產品組合升級與新增產能貢獻。
台灣廠持續將既有機台由一代轉向二代低介電布，東莞廠自 1Q27 起採台灣供
紗、當地織布模式，分批將 E 布產能轉作二代布；泰國一期則自 2H27 起逐
步投產，主要承接客戶對非中國高階產能的需求。隨三地產能同步往高階產品
配置，研究部預估二代低介電布營收占比將由 2026 年的 27% 提升至 2027 
年的 51%，成為獲利成長的主要來源。研究部預估 2027 年營收 149.82 億元
（+52.4% YoY） ，並隨泰國產能開出及二代布占比提升呈逐季成長；毛利率升
至 54.8%，營益率達 45.7%，帶動稅後淨利 57.18 億元（+81.4% YoY），EPS 
9.30 元。

2H26 受惠二代低介電布需求放
量 、 漲價效益與良率改善 ， 富喬營
收與毛利率同步攀升，研究部預
期 3Q26、4Q26 營運將連續改寫
新高。

2027 年富喬成長動能將由漲價
轉 向 產 品 組 合 升 級 與 新 產 能 貢
獻 ， 隨台灣 、 東莞及泰國產能加速
轉向二代低介電布，研究部預估
二代布營收占比升至 51%，帶動 
EPS 成長至 9.30 元。

投資建議 
研究部初次給予富喬買進投資評等，主要考量：(1) 石英布受鑽孔損耗與加
工良率限制 ， 導入時程遞延 ， 終端客戶轉回採用二代低介電布搭配高階樹脂的
架構，延長二代布產品週期，帶動富喬二代低介電布營收占比由 2025 年的 
7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 51%；(2) 玻纖布織布機由豐田獨
家供應，目前新機交期已排至 2031 年，意味 2027 年以前產業新增供給主
要仰賴既有機台重新配置 ， 富喬可在無須大幅增加資本支出的情況下 ， 持續將
既有產能由 E 布轉向一代及二代低介電布，研究部推算二代布毛利率較一代
布高逾 10 個百分點，產品組合優化將推升毛利率由 2025 年的 27.6%，提
升至 2026 年的 44.7% 與 2027 年的 54.8%；(3) 東莞廠自 1Q27 起採台灣
供紗、當地織布模式，分批將既有產能轉作二代低介電布，泰國一期則規劃  
500 台織機，自 2H27 起逐步開出，同樣以低介電布為主要產品，並承接客
戶對非中國產能的需求 。綜上所述 ， 研究部預估富喬 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 
分別為 5.13 元與 9.30 元，稅後純益分別年增 267.9% 與 81.4%。考量富
喬為日系龍頭以外少數具備紗、布垂直整合與自有配方 能力的供應商，以  
2027 年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 185 元，初次給予買
進投資評等。

## Document 078: 260920_citi_US-marketing-feedback

n
Vi e w p o i n t |

### Page 2

Taiwan Semiconductors
21 September 2026 Citi Research

TSMC: AI growth broadens beyond GPU/ ASIC  — TSMC remains central to nearly
every major AI hardware transition. The most important point is that its AI
opportunity is broader than the GPU/ASIC revenue definition investors typically
focus on. Networking silicon, AI CPUs, silicon photonics and advanced packaging
provide incremental growth vectors, while the underlying compute roadmap
continues migrating rapidly toward leading-edge nodes. We expect stronger 2027

growth for TSMC thanks to full-year contribution from 2nm, increased 3nm AI-
chip volumes and potential pricing increases, creating another powerful
combination of volume, mix and ASP expansion. In terms of competitive
landscape, we think Intel still needs substantial leading-edge capacity for its own
CPUs and appears constrained in its ability to simultaneously support internal
products and large external AI customers. In addition, Intel’s 14A ecosystem and

PDK/customer engagement process would require considerable qualification and
design-cycle time before translating into meaningful external production. For
critical AI products, customers also need predictable multiyear capacity
commitments; TSMC’s manufacturing scale and supply-chain coordination remain
important competitive advantages. Nevertheless, we see potential opportunities
for Intel and UMC’s collaboration at 12nm, that could support products such as

connectivity, display-driver or consumer-related chips, while its advanced
packaging technology could also win specialized programs. However, those
opportunities should be distinguished from a broad challenge to TSMC’s leading-
edge foundry position. Strategically, we think TSMC is becoming more selective
about where it deploys capital and engineering resources. We believe front-end
wafer manufacturing, SoIC and differentiated technologies such as silicon

photonics remain priorities, while more conventional back-end operations are
increasingly suitable for outsourcing. This suggests that continued AI growth can
benefit TSMC directly through leading-edge wafer revenue while simultaneously
creating substantial opportunities for OSAT partners.
TSMC outsourcing creates a structural opportunity for ASEH- Given the tight
supply for both advanced node wafer and CoWoS, we think leading-edge front-
end manufacturing generates the highest strategic value, and we believe TSMC has

an incentive to prioritize wafer fabrication and differentiated packaging
technologies such as SoIC while outsourcing larger portions of more back-end
processing. CoWoS capacity can continue expanding, but TSMC’s own back-end
investment intensity may eventually moderate as qualified OSAT partners assume
more steps. ASE/SPIL are already participating in substrate-related outsourced
work and should gain additional opportunities as the ecosystem matures. Other

Taiwanese packaging companies can also participate, while interposer production
itself could become more distributed across external partners. Importantly, ASEH
is not dependent solely on TSMC outsourcing. It is developing its own full-process
advanced-packaging capabilities, including FoCoS, providing a more direct route
to capture high-value AI packaging revenue. AMD is an important
customer/catalyst for ASEH, in our view, particularly as CPU and heterogeneous-

compute architectures require increasingly sophisticated packaging. ASEH’s
management’s expectation that leading-edge advanced-packaging revenue can
roughly double underscores the potential operating leverage. As chiplets, HBM,
silicon photonics and increasingly complex interconnects proliferate, packaging
content per device rises and the technical barriers to entry increase, potentially
supporting structurally better margins for ASEH.
CPO is emerging as the next major AI infrastructure transition - Co-packaged

optics (CPO) appears to be one of the clearest structural transitions in AI hardware
because it addresses two increasingly severe constraints simultaneously: data
transmission bandwidth and power consumption. We expect Nvidia should be the
initial catalyst, first through Spectrum-X CPO switches and subsequently
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 3

Taiwan Semiconductors
21 September 2026 Citi Research
3
through CPO integration associated with next-generation NVLink scale-up
architectures and Rubin Ultra. Importantly, there are two different CPO adoption
timelines that are often confused. CPO embedded directly alongside GPU/compute
packages could remain two to three years away, but switch-level CPO is
considerably closer and should begin scaling earlier. Moving optical connectivity
closer to the switching silicon can materially reduce electrical I/O distance and

power while supporting higher aggregate bandwidth. During Semicon Taiwan in
early Sep, CPO coupling, optical alignment, fiber-array assembly and testing were
among the most prominent themes. TSMC is developing its COUPE silicon-
photonics/packaging platform, while a broader ecosystem is forming around
optical engines, lasers, fiber-array units (FAUs), receptacles, precision alignment
and test equipment. The most visible near-term bottlenecks appear to be external

laser sources and fiber/FAU capacity, helping explain aggressive expansion by
suppliers such as Lumentum and Corning. The transition could also redistribute
semiconductor supply-chain value as CPO moves more value into advanced
semiconductor packaging, silicon photonics, optical coupling and testing. We
believe Largan’s high precision automation capability will be able to catch CPO
opportunity particularly in the most challenging optical fiber array alignment

process.
Custom ASIC: MediaTek and GUC riding with Google - The Google TPU ecosystem
illustrates both the opportunity and the execution complexity of custom AI silicon.
We believe MediaTek’s role should increase substantially over coming generations,
MediaTek’ s target market share is 15–20% initially but we see more upside ahead.
With current TPU v8 generation, MediaTek’s responsibility is concentrated more
heavily in I/O, SerDes and wafer-handling-related activities; in the subsequent

generation, its scope expands toward portions of the compute implementation as
well. The SerDes configuration itself is also expected to increase, while advanced-
node compute content materially lifts MediaTek’s dollar value per device.
Consequently, revenue can grow much faster than TPU units or market share alone
would suggest. One execution risk is advanced packaging. A future
Google/MediaTek program is expected to utilize Intel’s EMIB-T technology, which is

less proven at very high volume than TSMC’s mature CoWoS ecosystem. Much of
the difficult integration work, however, is distributed across substrate suppliers
and OSAT partners. We think the initial yields and volumes could therefore be
constrained, but the program has strategic importance for Google, Intel and
MediaTek and is likely to proceed despite an early learning curve. And we believe its
existing TPU generations should also continue shipping during the transition,

cushioning the revenue impact. We believe Google’s broader engagement with
AMD and Marvell should not automatically be interpreted as additional TPU
suppliers, in our view, Marvell has important networking and storage relationships
with Google, while AMD opportunities appear more closely associated with CPUs.
More broadly, the ASIC ecosystem remains structurally more fragmented than
Nvidia’s platform. Google is gradually internalizing more silicon expertise, but it still

requires external design-service and implementation partners. We also see
substantial growth of Google’s CPU at GUC, which is fully COT model with back-
end only service.
AI systems and power: Hon Hai benefits from Rubin, while 800VDC adoption
should be gradual- At the system level, Hon Hai remains a major beneficiary of
Nvidia’s rack-scale transition. This year’s growth has been driven largely by higher
AI rack shipments, but next year adds a much higher ASP as the supply chain

transitions from GB300 toward Vera Rubin. Higher rack value means revenue and
profit can rise substantially even if unit growth moderates, provided margins
remain broadly stable. Beyond Nvidia, Hon Hai is also positioning itself to
manufacture custom-ASIC and hyperscaler systems, including CPU- and TPU-
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Taiwan Semiconductors
21 September 2026 Citi Research
4
related racks, which could begin contributing more meaningfully later next year.
Power architecture is another major debate, particularly around 800VDC. The
key distinction is that 800VDC is strategically attractive but not necessarily
mandatory for Rubin or even every Rubin Ultra deployment. Rack power rises
materially from the GB300 generation toward Rubin, requiring changes such as
sidecar power shelves and more sophisticated power distribution. New greenfield

AI data centers and neocloud deployments have stronger incentives to design
around 800VDC because they can optimize the entire facility for future high-
density racks. Existing facilities, however, can continue using alternative
architectures, making adoption gradual rather than immediate. We believe Delta
remains structurally well positioned in high-power data-center infrastructure, but
expectations around the speed of 800VDC adoption may be slower than expected.

The industry also faces a significant engineering learning curve as rack power
density rises, including thermal management and potential overheating issues.
Separately, shortages of power-management ICs and capacitors remain important
constraints regardless of whether a data center uses 800VDC. Analog and power-
semiconductor supply is supported by both AI-driven demand and relatively tight
capacity at IDM suppliers and specialty foundries such as UMC and VIS.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
CCL –  Doosan announces
capacity expansion in Korea and
China
What’s new? According to Business Korea, Doosan (000150.KS, not covered)
announced a KRW970B CCL capacity expansion on Sep 17:
• KRW420B in Korea to add optical module CCL lines, and
• KRW550B in China to build a new plant for AI accelerator and network
switch CCL.
Spending is phased through 2028, with the new lines coming online sequentially

from 2H28.
Our thoughts:  Our checks suggest this investment could add ~2,000K sheets per
month of CCL capacity at Doosan, equivalent to ~2% of global CCL capacity in 2028,
on our estimates. We view the investment as further evidence of robust demand for
high-end CCL and do not believe it undermines our thesis of continued high-end CCL
scarcity (see our CCL initiation report). As such, we see a positive read-across for
EMC, TUC, and Shengyi. We remain OW on EMC and TUC, while maintaining our EW

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 20, 2026 07:52 PM GMT

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
TFT-LCD Panel Prices for Sep-
26: TVs, Monitors and NBs Flat
MoM
Chinese panel makers are planning to raise panel prices in the
coming months to mitigate the cost pressure, which we believe
could find some resistance among their brand customers. Glass
opportunities in advanced packaging remain the key reasons that
major panel makers are trading at 1.5-2.0x P/B.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

75" 4K 65" 4K 55" FHD 50" FHD 43" FHD 32" HD
Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices stayed flat MoM in September: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and
75" TV panel prices all stood flat for the month of September,  in line with our
expectations. While the overall TV panel demand was lackluster, there is still some
seasonal demand as brands are preparing for year-end promotions. Recently, the
major Chinese panel makers have notified their customers of price hikes from

September/October in order to mitigate increasing margin pressure caused by rising
costs of various components, including driver ICs, glass substrates and PCB.
Although major Chinese panel makers might leverage their annual maintenance of
three to seven days during October 2026 as a bargaining chip, we think it might take
more negotiations with brand customers before they can really raise panel prices,
since order momentum will slow down in the coming months. On a quarterly

average basis, TV panel prices  declined in the low-single-digits % QoQ in 3Q26.
IT panel prices were flat MoM in  September: Monitor and notebook panel prices
stayed flat MoM during the month. Both applications continues to  see slower
demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about  increasing
component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and reluctant to
given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect IT panel

prices to remain flat QoQ in 3Q26.
Risk/reward less attractive: We think that the current valuation of Taiwanese panel
makers, AUO (2409.TW) and Innolux (3481.TW) both at 1.7x P/B, has already baked
in some expectations of glass in advanced packaging for revenue contribution in
2028. However, some supply chain players think this might take longer, and there
seem to be more suppliers from different supply chains vying for this opportunity. In
China, while expectations are also building for the glass core substrate, we have a

relative preference for BOE (000725.SZ; 1.5x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.8x P/B),
on its scale advantage and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Current valuation of 1.5-2.0x P/B have already priced in the expectation of glass
in advanced packaging from 2H28.
Nonetheless,  the risk in commercialization and competition are worth monitoring.

TV Panel Prices Trending Down from 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, and September was flat MoM,   in line
with our expectation that TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That
said, we do not expect a significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide

downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  at ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance

following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.
Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing

structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to
profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

### Page 5

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Fair After Recent Correction
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.6x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.8x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end demand: If end demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What’s new? Kinsus (3189.TW, not covered) reported August 2026 profit per TWSE
regulatory requirements:
• August revenue was NT$4,810M (+6% m/m, +41% y/y). July and August
revenues accounted for 68% of the consensus 3Q revenue estimate.
• August pre-tax profit was NT$1,110M (+256% y/y), 51% of the consensus 3Q
pre-tax profit forecast.
• August net profit was NT$817M (+354% y/y), 55% of the consensus 3Q net

profit estimate.
Our thoughts:
Kinsus is one of the leading global IC substrate suppliers, with exposure to both ABF
and BT substrates and the highest BT revenue mix among Taiwanese substrate
peers. We see an encouraging read-through to Unimicron and NYPCB, both rated
OW, to the extent that the strength reflects improving underlying substrate
profitability. The results are consistent with our constructive view of ABF substrate
makers and continued margin improvement.

Exhibit 1: Kinsus' preliminary August 2026 profit numbers

investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
August pre-tax margin reached 23.1%, vs. 14.3% in 2Q26.
With one month’s net profit equivalent to more than half of consensus quarterly
earnings, the disclosure suggests upside risk to Kinsus’ 3Q consensus estimates.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers
n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)

Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand

n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Hon Hai (2317.TW): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 3Q 
/ 4Q26E; 3-month revenues preview; Buy (on CL)
24 September 2026 | 8:50PM HKT
We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +14% / +23% QoQ, driven by (1) AI 
data center: The next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp up, with 
higher ASP and early delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first one to deliver the 
latest model, around 2-3 months ahead of peers, driving its Sep and onward revenue 
growth. Followed by GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks 
would fuel the growth in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27, and 
(2) Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along 
with the new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlight in September 
(report link), we reduce global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m to 
1.1bn / 1.2bn/ 1.2bn in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; 
however, we remain positive on global leading brands to outperform the market 
given the strong brand awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ 
stronger consumption power. We remain positive on foldable phones and expect 
premium models to continue gain market share, positive to Hon Hai. Maintain Buy 
(on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect company’s 3Q26E revenues to be +40% 
YoY/ +14% QoQ to NT$2.9 trillion. Both Jul and Aug delivered strong revenues YoY 
growth (+54%/ 52% YoY), and we expect to see both YoY and MoM positive growth 
in Sep to Dec, driven by new AI server racks, CPO switch, and latest smartphone 
models and foldable phones. We keep 2026E - 2028E earnings estimates largely 
unchanged.

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and we continue to 
derive it through near term PE. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 17.9x (vs. 17.8x of our 
previous TP implied 2027E P/E) is derived from peers’ average ratio of trading P/E to 
forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the sum of NI YoY and OPM). Our 
target P/E of 17.9x is in the range of the company’s historical forward P/E since 2010, 
reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business expansion from the competitive 
consumer electronics market to AI servers and EVs outsourcing, which should drive 
profit expansion with diversifying business opportunities for Hon Hai. Maintain Buy (on 
CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM).

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

## Document 105: 260928_ubs_n2-TAM

Source PDF: all report pdf/260928_ubs_n2-TAM.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 16

### Page 1

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
UBS Global I/O Semiconductors
Foundry: Much larger N2 TAM and faster A14
expansion; mature foundry upcycle underway
N2 demand inflection larger than expected
Our proprietary bottoms-up N2 foundry TAM analysis points to substantially larger
demand of 320kwpm by 2028E, versus our prior estimate of approximately 230kwpm a
year ago. The increase is driven primarily by stronger server CPU demand and
accelerating AI compute deployments, particularly as a growing number of ASIC

programs ramp into volume production alongside GPUs over the next several years. At
the same time, we  expect Intel to migrate a larger portion of its PC CPU production to
18A as execution improves. As a result, we estimate server CPUs and accelerators will
account for 54% of N2 foundry demand by 2028, up from our previous estimate of
44%, while PCs should contribute 19% and smartphones/tablets 26%. (See Figure 6Q u a n t if y in g  N 2  d e m a n d  o p p o r t u n it ie s  f o r  f o u n d r y  -

Figure 7N 2  f o u n d r y  d e m a n d  b y  a p p lic a t io n  (2 0 2 8 E )). The larger N2 opportunity supports robust revenue growth for TSMC through
2028-29E as the industry's major foundry supplier, even as competition from Samsung
Foundry, Intel, and Terafab increases modestly.
Further acceleration of N3/2 expansion; quicker A14 capacity build-up
To meet the larger N2 demand outlook, we raise our estimate for industry N2 capacity at
end-2028 to 312kwpm, up from 230kwpm previously. We forecast TSMC's N2 capacity

reaching 210kwpm in 2028, versus our prior estimate of 160kwpm. Intel should also
scale capacity more aggressively, exceeding 60kwpm in 2028E for internal CPU
production, compared with our previous forecast of 40kwpm. Meanwhile,
strengthening cloud AI demand in 2027-28E is pushing our industry N3 capacity
forecast to 290-300kwpm by 2028, up from 270kwpm previously, driven entirely by
faster expansion at TSMC. TSMC's A14 node remains on track for mass production in

2028, supported by steady R&D progress and growing customer engagement. Industry
feedback suggests TSMC is pulling forward its expansion schedule, targeting
approximately 60kwpm of A14 capacity in 2028 when the node enters mass
production, based on  UBSe. Reflecting the more aggressive leading-edge expansion, we
raise our TSMC capex forecast to US$90bn in 2027 and US$105bn in 2028, up from US
$63bn this year. See our Q326 earnings preview.
Mature foundry: favorable supply/demand and pricing outlook in 2027-28

We were early in advocating the anti-consensus view of a mature foundry upcycle,
driven by an industry-wide supply and resource reallocation. Utilization and pricing
trends have continued to improve throughout 2026, and we expect more meaningful
earnings upside in 2027-28. Our updated analysis suggests 8-inch foundry utilization
could rise to 95% in 2027, up from 85% in 2026, supported by growing server PMIC
demand. While 12-inch 28/40nm demand may face near-term headwinds from weaker

smartphone and consumer demand, we still expect utilization to improve to 88% in
2027 from 84% in 2026, supported by modest capacity growth. We also believe
competitive behaviour from Chinese foundries should remain rational into 2027, as
major players increasingly prioritize capital allocation toward leading-edge technologies.
Stock recommendations
We reiterate Buy on TSMC and expect further upward revisions to Street revenue, EPS
and capex estimates for 2027-28. Within mature foundry, UMC remains our top pick, as

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
The semiconductor industry is cyclically sensitive to the economy and so upside and downside
risks relate to macro drivers. Other, semiconductor capital equipment specific downside risks
include the rapid loss of market share due to product timing/performance , design or
distribution. The supply chain companies into the handset industry also operate in a highly
cyclical market both from macro concerns and inventory cycles within the supply chain. These

companies also operate in a highly competitive environment with product innovation and
R&D investments critical to success.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

## Document 107: 260928_ubs_TSMC

### Page 1

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing
Q326 earnings preview: Raising 2027-28E capex
for next phase of AI growth
Cloud AI mega-trend driving sales growth >40% in 2026E and 40% in 2027E
Feedback from our recent industry analysis suggests no signs of Cloud AI demand
moderation (Summit Day 1, Day 2&3, CoWoS update); yet the demand outlook has
further strengthened across CPUs, GPUs and ASICs in 2027-28. TSMC is on track to

reach 2026 sales guidance of slightly over 40% growth in USD (UBSe 41%), and our
growth analysis points to sustained 40% sales growth in 2027E, above consensus of
36%. We estimate Q3 sales at 14% QoQ in USD and GM at 67.1%, both at high-end of
guidance. We forecast Q4 sales to grow 10% QoQ thanks to seasonal build of flagship
phones and accelerating ramp of high performance compute (HPC), with flat GM of
67.0% considering the dilution of N2 and overseas expansion.

Further step-up of capex in 2027-28E
We have been constructive about TSMC's capex and view higher capex essential in
solidifying TSMC's leadership in advanced foundry. We keep 2026E capex at US$63bn,
and raise 2027/28E capex to US$90bn/105bn from US$80bn/95bn previously. Higher
estimates are based on: 1) our industry analysis shows that TSMC is further accelerating
expansion across N3/2 and  pulls forward tool delivery schedule of A14 for larger capacity

by 2028 when in mass production; 2) more meaningful price inflation of equipment,
and required prepayment; 3) higher capex for advanced packaging. We forecast capital
intensity to rise moderately from 36.6% in 2026 to 37.4% in 2027 but rnomalize to
32.6% in 2028. We lift TSMC's N3/2 capacity estimates at end of 2028 to
220/210kwpm, vs 200/160kwpm prior. See our foundry sector note for details.
What will the earnings call focus on?
1) Overall semi cycle outlook. UBS forecasts semis (ex memory) revenue to grow

31%/15% in 2026/27 led by Cloud AI. UBS remains conservative on consumer end
demand, estimating further 3%/4% declines for smartphone/PC units in 2027. 2)
TSMC's capex strategy over the next 3-4 years,  and whether any bottlenecks could
constrain its expansion plans. 3) Competition, especially as peers such as Samsung
Foundry and Terafab have shown increased ability to invest, although we also believe
TSMC may engage for potential angles to collaborate on Terafab. 4) Gross margin in

H226/2027. We forecast GM to decline slightly  to 67.0% in H226, and stabilize at
67.2% in 2027, underpinned by price hike, scale benefits and improved product mix.
Valuation: reiterate Buy and target of NT$3,650
We raise 2027E and beyond capex  and lift long-term earnings CAGR from 25% to 27%.
Our target of NT$3,650 is based on 21x 2027-28E PE. We see meaningful re-rating
potential from the current 16.6x/12.2x 2027/28E PE . Key catalysts include stronger

2027-28E growth outlook, continued TAM expansion and higher capex spend.

Company Description
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing (TSMC) is the world's leading dedicated foundry
service provider, with annual capacity of about 32m of 8-inch equivalent wafers as of end-
2024. It operates four 12-inch wafer fabs, four 8-inch wafer fabs and one 6-inch wafer fab in

Taiwan. TSMC also manages two 8-inch fabs and one 12-inch fab overseas under wholly
owned subsidiaries WaferTech in the US, and TSMC China and TSMC Nanjing in China.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target for TSMC on a PE multiple.
We think TSMC faces a variety of risks, including rapidly changing technology, intense
competition, high capital investment and cyclical demand. TSMC also has a history of paying
stock options to its employees, which could dilute earnings.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

### Page 2

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Gold Circuit Electronics
Valuation
Our TP of NT$1,630 for GCE is based on 25x our 2027E EPS estimate, which is higher than 22x or +2std level of its 1-year-
forward PE over the past three years. We believe the 25x multiple is justified by GCE's high AI server/networking sales
exposure, market share gain in existing ASIC customer and new AI ASIC customer wins from 4Q26, which drives its AI ASIC

sales growth and margin profile. Recall that TSMC expects close to a 50%+ CAGR in the next five years for its AI-related
demand, which is stronger than any other applications. Thus, we believe investors would be willing to pay for high-growth AI
names (especially ASIC) with high PE multiples. Our target price is equivalent to 44x/25x our 2026/27E EPS estimates and
16.5x/9.9x our 2026/27E BVPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected server

recovery; 2) slower ramp-up pace of new server platform; 3) less-than-expected AI demand; 4) more PCB peers added into AI
supply chain; and 5) stricter practice of China+1 by customers due to geopolitical concerns.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

### Page 3

NYPCB (8046.TW)
30 September 2026 Citi Research
3
BT substrate: improving industrywide supply with sustainable memory demand — In
the near term, while we do observe fading BT demand from smartphone, we also see
robust memory demand likely help offset the weakness in smartphone. T o note that,
NYPCB’s memory sales mix has increased to >60% currently from 30% in the past.
Although the market was disappointed about NYPCB’s muted BT price hike in 4Q26,

we think its BT demand, especially from memory products, is likely to be more resilient
than expected, with potential upsides in 4Q26E or 1Q27E. In the medium term, we also
observe Unimicron and SEMCO (report here) are converting its BT capacity into ABF
one, which would gradually benefit the industrywide BT supply side from 4Q26
onward. More meaningful outflowing BT orders would likely occur after 2Q27 with the
last BT orders placed by customers to Unimicron and SEMCO.

Demand continues to build up; upward earnings revisions; Buy — We see
favorable ABF/BT trend for NYPCB in the upcoming quarters. For its PCB segment
(with demand mostly from non-AI applications now), it seems to be stabilizing in
the past few quarters and likely sees some upsides from new applications. Thus,
we raise our 2026/27 /28E earnings by 2%/8%/6% to factor better demand and
price hikes on supply tightness. We raise our TP to NT$1,700 (30x 2027E EPS) from

NT$1,600 (30x 2027E EPS) and reiterate our Buy rating. We also open 90-day
upside CW for NYPCB, as we expect AI chip production ramp along with price hikes
would drive NYPCB upcoming monthly sales momentum in 4Q26E.

NYPCB (8046.TW)

NYPCB
(8046.TW; NT$ 1340.0; 1; 30 Sep 26; 13:30)
Company description
Established in 1997, Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board (NYPCB) is a technology
subsidiary of the Formosa Plastics Group. Nan Ya PCB's main products
include conventional PCB, HDI, Flip Chip substrate (FCBGA and FCCSP), Wire
Bond Substrate, etc. The company has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
Kunshan, China.
Investment strategy
We rate NYPCB shares as Buy. We believe ABF/BT should see a solid recovery

in 2026 on robust AI demand: 1) NYPCB is the purest ABF play with a
significant earnings turnaround, driven by growing chip sizes and layer counts
for AI ASIC/networking; and 2) the BT business should benefit from price
hikes on rising memory demand and T glass shortage. Considering T glass
support from NYPCB’s parent company, we believe NYPCB would  have a
better chance to win additional AI ASIC/networking business going forward.
Valuation
Our DCF-based TP for NYPCB is NT$1,700. With a risk-free rate of 5.00% (10-

year government bond rate), a market risk premium of 7% and an equity beta
of 1.22 (YTD weekly share price), we calculate NYPCB's WACC as 13.4%. Our
TP is equivalent to 30x/13x 2027E EPS/BVPS, which reflects its high P/E
multiple during upcycles. NYPCB traded at 15-20x P/E during its 2003-2008
upcycle driven by PC/NB demand strength. In 2010-11 when the market was
recovering from the global financial crisis, NYPCB traded at 25-35x P/E. In the
last 2019-22 upcycle when ABF was in shortage, NYPCB traded at 30-40x

P/E.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the NYPCB shares from reaching our
target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected ABF demand from key
customers; 2) BT business seasonality weakness and fewer-than-expected
price hikes; and 3) severe T glass shortages that cause a bottleneck to its
production.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

E Ink (8069.TWO): E-paper application continues to expand; expect 
modest consumer electronics mix; Aug beat; Neutral
30 September 2026 | 3:59PM HKT
We remain positive on growing e-paper applications across e-readers, e-notes, and 
e-labels. However, consumer electronics end-demand could remain modest amid 
high memory costs. We expect E Ink’s consumer electronics (e-readers, e-notes) to 
decline 13% YoY in 2026E (vs. +6% YoY in 2025), and recover back to +12-16% YoY 
in 2027E-28E; on IoT applications (ESL and Signage), we expect revenue to grow at a 
+32% CAGR in 2026E-28E (vs. +22% YoY in 2025), driven by rising adoption on 
savings of labour costs, electricity, and paper, digitalization trend, and growing ESG 
awareness. Signage new products could also drive revenue growth with high ASPs, 
despite lower GMs considering in module format to accelerate customers’ adoption. 
Our target P/E multiple declines to 11x (vs. 13x previous TP implied 2027E P/E) on 
consumer electronics sector de-rating under high memory costs. Our new 12m TP is 
NT$142 (vs. NT$153 previously). Maintain Neutral.

3-month revenues preview: We expect a sequential increase in revenues through 
Sep-Dec, driven by e-labels, Signage ramp up, and better consumer electronics 
seasonality. The ongoing digitalization should support e-paper adoption; E Ink’s 
e-labels further penetrated in Canada, serving Sobeys (retail) to deploy ESLs across 
300 to 350 stores over the next 18 months (link). Overall, we expect 3Q26E / 4Q26E 
revenues to grow at +0% / +16% QoQ on the mix of modest consumer electronics 
end-demand under high memory costs, and ongoing e-labels adoption and Signage 
shipment ramp up.

### Page 2

Earnings revision

We factor in 2Q26 and Aug results and revise up our 2026E-28E net income by 15% / 
10% / 8% mainly on higher gross margins, given better product mix (e-labels mainly 
delivered via e-paper while consumer electronics mainly via module, carry a lower GM). 
Overall, we expect 2026E revenues growth at +13% YoY (vs. +12% YoY in 2025A), driven 
by e-labels growing application and adoption, which we model to grow +47% YoY (vs. 
+22% YoY in 2025A), while we also expect a decline in consumer electronics under high 
memory costs (-13% YoY vs. +6% YoY in 2025). We continue to expect revenue growth in 
2027E-28E driven by (1) rising color e-notes / e-readers enlarging size, (2) more stores 
adopting e-labels to save operation costs with growing outdoor signage. On GM, we 
increase our forecasts in 2026E-28E to reflect 2Q26 beat on better product mix.

Valuation

We continue to derive our 12m TP based on a near-term P/E. We derive our target P/E 
via E Ink’s avg. 12m forward PEG ratio since 2017. With largely the same PEG ratio of 
0.5x, 22% forward year earnings growth (vs. 26% previously), our new target P/E is at 
11x (vs. 13x our TP implied 2027E P/E previously). The new target P/E of 11x is largely in 
line with the company’s avg.-1stv. P/E, reflecting the consumer electronics sector 
de-rating under high memory costs. Our new 12m target price is NT$142 (vs. NT$153 
previously). Maintain Neutral.

### Page 4

Price target methodology and risks - E Ink 
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral rated on E Ink. Our 12m target price for E Ink is 
based on a near-term P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. Our 12-m 
TP of NT$142 is based on a target P/E multiple of 11x forward year EPS (2027E). Our 
target P/E multiple of 11x is derived from the company’s avg. PEG since 2017 (0.5x), as 
we expect macro uncertainties to reduce the visibility of e-paper adoption, while we 
remain positive in the long term: technology upgrades (colour, larger sizes) to unlock the 
e-paper market potential. 
Upside / Downside risks: (1) Faster- / slower-than-expected e-readers / e-notes 
penetration rate, (2) Faster- / slower-than-expected ESL adoption rate, (3) Fiercer- / 
healthier-than-expected competition, which could be driven by competition from other 
technology gadgets (e.g. AI / AR glasses, AI PCs, tablets, smart watches, etc.) or new 
entrants in e-paper industry.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

研究部維持奇鋐買進投資建議，並上修目標價，主係：(1) 4Q26 隨著
VR200 小量出貨，AWS T3 水冷及Google TPU 同步放量，營收與獲利
將逐季走高；(2)預期 AWS 及 AMD 將採用雙面水冷版，單價大幅提升；
(3)ASIC 產品毛利率高於GPU， 且預估27 年水冷營收比重將超過 5 成；
(4) NVIDIA CDU 認證名單及 Switch tray 規格的市場雜音，對基本
面影響有限。綜上所述，研究部上修 27 年營收至 3,409 億元(原估
3,144 億元)，EPS 上修至 180.69 元(原估 162.71 元)，給予 25 倍本
益比，目標價上修至 4,500 元，維持買進建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 4Q隨著VR小批量出貨，兩家ASIC放量，營運持續季增 
隨著GB300出貨持續強勁 ，VR小批量出貨， 研究部預期奇鋐9月
營收持續月增。展望4Q26，除了VR200板端開始小量出貨外，
Google TPU以及AWS T3水冷也將在4Q開始陸續放量，研究部微
幅調整4Q26營收至632億元(原估622億元)，季增11.9%，年增
32.3%。而VR200的出貨將持續到2Q27，3Q27將開始有小量Rubin 
ultra產品接續。

2. AWS及AMD採用雙面水冷板，散熱產值增加 
根據供應鏈調查， AWS T3水冷以及AMD MI系列將採用雙面式
水冷板，主因採用背面供電(主板空間不夠，記憶體模組移到
主板背面，故兩面都需水冷板散熱)，雙面式水冷板單價將有
望超過3000美元，產值大幅提升。此外，ASIC產品毛利率優於
GPU，公司產能也優先配置給ASIC，故研究部上修27年營收至
3409億元。

3. CDU認證及switch tray規格雜音，對基本面影響極小 
近期散熱有部分雜音：(1) NVIDIA DSX Ready 的CDU認證裡，
取得資格的業者為 LG Electronics、LiquidStack與Vertiv三
間，奇鋐未在首批名單中。研究部認為，目前奇鋐目前主力產
品仍為水冷板等零組件，CDU比較偏向系統級產品 ，27-28年才
會開始小量生產CDU ( 以In row 架構為主)，但目前營收比重
低，影響不大。(2)傳言27年Rubin Ultra 的Switch tray將從
18層改回9層設計，目前仍在討論中，但研究部 對奇鋐的27年
EPS預估，本身就未納入18層規格。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

4. 上修27年EPS預估，目標價上修至4500元  
除了GPU及ASIC需要水冷板之外，周圍相關產品也開始逐漸導
入液冷，如CPU/LPU Rack 、CPO等，奇鋐預估27年水冷營收比
重將超過5成。綜上所述 ，研究部微幅上修2026年營收至2,178
億元(原估2,168億元)， 年增56.0%；EPS 104.65元(原估104.02
元)，並上修2027年營收至3,409億元(原估3,144億元)，年增
56.5%，EPS上修至180.69元(原估162.71元)，給予25倍本益比 ，
目標價上修至4,500元，維持買進建議。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

新日興 (3376 TT)  Trading Buy

低軌道衛星、 AI 伺服器水冷散熱零組件為未來成長動能。  
投資建議 ：折疊式螢幕手機 Hinge 於 2H26 有獲利貢獻， AI 伺
服器水冷散熱零組件 2027 年將有營收挹注 ， 投資評等為Trading 
Buy ，目標價為 203 元。  
預估 NB Hinge 2026 年營收與 2025 年持平： 營運重心原以某美
系客戶產品為主，故於 NB Hinge 市佔率逐年下滑。受需求不佳
影響， NB Hinge 2025 年營收 YoY+5.1% 。為分散營運風險，公
司積極爭取其他 NB 品牌訂單，但受 NB 市況不佳影響，預估
2026 年 NB Hinge 營收與 2025 年持平。  
穿戴式產品預估 2026 年營收 YoY-17.6% ：穿戴式產品包含第
二代一般型充電盒 Hinge 、高階版 TWS 充電盒 Hinge 、耳罩式
耳機 Hinge 等。因 2024 年基期較高， 2025 年穿戴式產品營收
YoY-28.8% 。受消費市場不佳影響，預估 2026 年穿戴式產品營
收 YoY-17.6% 。  
折疊式螢幕手機 Hinge 於 2H26 有獲利貢獻： 新日興於折疊螢
幕式產品 Hinge 與美系客戶已開發多年，此美系客戶已於 3Q26
發布其首款折疊式螢幕手機，預估折疊式螢幕手機 Hinge 於
2H26 年有獲利貢獻，但較預期為低 。 
預期 AI 伺服器水冷散熱零組件 2027 年將有營收挹注： 新日興
的精密加工設備主要在台灣，近年開拓精密零件領域業務，已
取得低軌道衛星零件、電動自行車精密零件、光學檢測儀器零
件、模治具零件等訂單，營收逐年提升中。低軌道衛星零件已
量產出貨，取得品項亦逐步增加中。  
新日興有 CNC 加工、雷射焊接、雷射切割多年製造經驗，亦積
極切入 AI 伺服器水冷散熱領域。已與多家客戶洽談分歧管代工
業務，目前產品認證中，預期 4Q26 有機會少量出貨 ， 2027 年
有較顯著營收貢獻 ，未來將爭取快速接頭代工訂單 。

### Page 6

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 22 日

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

彰濱廠與嘉興廠需求回升帶動產能滿載，3Q26 獲利延續成長 
2Q26 台灣營收季增 23%、中國營收季增 56%，受惠台灣半導體先進製程及
化學品供應鏈擴廠需求延續，台灣廠既有產能維持滿載；中國半導體及記憶
體廠擴產亦帶動桶槽設備拉貨回升，嘉興廠稼動率提升至 95%-100%，EPS
季增 23.4%至 3.11 元。3Q26 隨台灣擴產效益逐步顯現、稼動率提升及訂單
組合改善，預估毛利率季增 0.7ppts 至 36.3%，惟仍受先前承接的低毛利訂
單影響，改善幅度相對溫和，EPS 估季增 29.3%至 4.02 元。 
訂單能見度至 2028 年，備貨與擴產支撐 2026-2027 年成長 
觀察上品 1) 3Q26 在手訂單由年初 38 億回升至 75 億台幣，接單動能明顯轉
強；2) 2Q26 存貨季增 21.5%至 20.52 億元，其中商品及製成品季增 24.7% 
(佔比提升至 44%)，反應部分訂單已完成生產，目前正進行客戶廠內驗收，
待驗收完成並依約交貨後，才陸續認列營收，且存貨周轉天數未伴隨備貨明
顯惡化。本輪訂單、備貨及產能利用率同步回升，配合 1H27 產能排程已滿
與擴產規劃，訂單能見度到 2028 年，且台灣/中國/美國晶圓/化學品廠積極
擴廠計畫，我們認為將支持 2H26-2027 年營收與獲利成長，估 2026/2027
年 EPS 將年增 39.3%/52.8%至 14.21/21.72 元。 
給予上品買進評等，目標價 350 元 
中國市場雖仍面臨價格競爭，需求回升已帶動嘉興廠滿載，台灣晶圓廠與化
學品廠亦持續擴產，我們認為上品憑藉氟樹脂加工、設備整合及客戶認證優
勢，可望掌握建廠商機，持續支持營收與獲利成長。評價方面， 參考上品過
去三年本益比多落在 11-19 倍、同業平均本益比約 13-18 倍，考量上品具較
佳的獲利能力與 2027 年獲利成長潛力，且訂單能見度至 2028 年，我們認
為目前評價尚未充分反應擴產及毛利率修復效益，因此給予 16 倍本益比，
以 2027 年 EPS 21.72 元，推得目標價 350 元，給予買進評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 台灣半導體先進製程及化學品供應鏈擴廠需求延續、中國半導體
及記憶體廠擴產亦帶動拉貨回升，彰濱/嘉興稼動率皆滿載。 
⧫  
在手訂單由年初 38 億回升至 75 億台幣，接單動能明顯轉強；2Q26 存
貨季增 22%，其中商品及製成品季增 25%，營收將陸續貢獻。 
⧫  
考量上品具較佳的獲利能力與 2027 年獲利成長潛力，且訂單能見度至
2028 年，以 2027 年 EPS、16 倍本益比，推得目標價 350 元。

2) 中國地區： 
2Q26 中國營收季增 56%/年減 22%至 5.8 億元，年減受 2Q25 建廠案件密集 貨的高基期影響。
隨當地晶圓代工、記憶體及電子化學品廠需求改善，目前嘉興既有產線已滿載，產能排程延伸
至 2026 年底，且管理層預估 2026 年中國接單金額將年增 40%。考量當地訂單交期通常約 4
個月，能見度較台灣短，加上市場價格競爭仍激烈，後續將優先挑選利潤較佳案件。我們認為
接單回升與既有產線滿載有利後續 貨及固定成本分攤，惟獲利改善幅度仍取決於接單價格與
產品組合。

稼動率回升與訂單組合改善，推動 2026-2027 年獲利逐步修復 
上品毛利率於 2024-2025 年受價格競爭、產品組合轉差及產能利用率下降影響而承壓，2026 年仍
受前一年承接的低毛利台灣訂單拖累，相關訂單預計交付至 2027 年初。若依期完成，隨較佳定價
的新訂單陸續認列，毛利率壓力有望逐步緩解。觀察 1) 2Q26 彰濱既有產能已滿載、嘉興稼動率回
升至 95%-100%，隨後續 貨認列，產能利用率改善效益有望逐步反映於營收與獲利； 2) 在手訂
單回升至 75 億元，其中約 80%來自毛利率相對較高的台灣業務，後續交付有望帶動地區組合改善。

圖 11：兩廠稼動率回升，毛利率仍受低價舊單壓抑，後續改善可期

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧預估 
此外，材料成本主要受 PTFE/PFA 以及白鐵價格影響，公司採 專案報價，依接單當時的材料、人
工及製造成本估算，並預留成本波動空間，同時透過與供應商協調、提前安排採購，以及調整新訂
單報價，降低原料漲價衝擊。上品直接向上游原料廠採購，亦具相對有利的貨源取得條件，然設備
接單至交付存在時間差，若既有訂單售價已確定、後續採購成本持續上升，仍可能壓縮專案毛利，
因此隨新訂單逐步反應最新成本，材料漲價壓力有望緩解 。另外，先前已提列跌價的存貨，若淨變
現價值隨材料價格回升，亦有機會認列回沖利益，提供額外獲利貢獻 。整體而言，我們認為 2026
年營運已進入修復階段， 2027 年則有望更完整反映新增產能及訂單組合改善效益，支持獲利持續
成長，估 2026/2027 年毛利率將年增 3.1ppts/3.7ppts 至 36.1%/39.8%。
